# titeZMe — Typography

Audit date: 2026-10-01 (Europe/Madrid). Existing implementation only. Source occurrences do not establish live data, runtime reachability or design intent. No production code changed.

## Fonts loaded versus used


| Family/source | Weights/styles | Loading/application |
|---|---|---|
| Nunito/next-font-google | Weight option omitted: variable Google defaults; no italic requested; inline800/900. | --font-nunito variable on html; no --font-sans binding. Body font-sans computes system stack in screenshots. Inline Wordmark/dialog/toast/marketing/admin request Nunito. [app/layout.tsx:1](../app/layout.tsx#L1); [components/Wordmark.tsx:1](../components/Wordmark.tsx#L1) |
| Cormorant Garamond/next-font-google | Explicit300/400/500/600; no italic requested. | Only protected Titiza route loads --font-titiza-serif. [app/dashboard/client/titiza/page.tsx:1](../app/dashboard/client/titiza/page.tsx#L1) |
| System sans | ui-sans-serif/system-ui/sans-serif + Apple Color Emoji/Segoe UI Emoji/Segoe UI Symbol/Noto Color Emoji. | Tailwind default/runtime body; exact selected OS face: Not established / not found. |
| Serif fallback | ui-serif/Georgia/Times New Roman/serif. | Global --font-serif; public film lacks route-scoped Cormorant variable. |
| Mono fallback | ui-monospace/SFMono-Regular/Menlo/Monaco/Consolas/Liberation Mono/Courier New/monospace. | font-mono labels/timeline/numbers; no downloaded mono face. |
| Emoji | OS emoji fallbacks. | Icons/flags; platform-dependent. |


## Roles; no explicit unified typography system


| Role | Existing styling |
|---|---|
| Landing h1 | 6xl→md88px/black/leading.95/tracking-tight. [app/LandingPageClient.tsx:90](../app/LandingPageClient.tsx#L90) |
| Discovery | 4xl→md5xl black. [app/shops/page.tsx:313](../app/shops/page.tsx#L313) |
| Auth | Login32px/signup28px black. [app/login/page.tsx:220](../app/login/page.tsx#L220); [app/signup/page.tsx:265](../app/signup/page.tsx#L265) |
| Dashboard/admin | 2xlblack,billingbold;admin28px/900. |
| Titiza | Seriflight:title6xl→sm7xl→md8xl,greeting3xl→sm4xl. [components/titiza/titiza-hero.tsx:1](../components/titiza/titiza-hero.tsx#L1) |
| Body | sm/base/lg/inline13px; marketplacebold/Titizalight, leading-relaxed/1.6. |
| Labels | 10/11/12pxuppercase,bold/extrabold/medium,tracking-widest/.2/.3/.35em. |
| Buttons | Marketplaceblack/extrabold13/15px,Titizasmmediumuppercase/.2–.25em. No common Button API. |
| Navigation | TopNav14pxbold,sidebar13pxbold,mobile10px. |
| Statistics | Landing32pxblack,decorative180px,local chart ticks. |
| Inputs | Global16px!important overrides smaller input declarations. [app/globals.css:77](../app/globals.css#L77) |
| Transform/style | Local italic ComingSoon/uppercase/truncate/line-clamp; no common semantic definitions. |


## Default exact typography values


| Token | Installed definition |
|---|---|
| --text-xs | 0.75rem |
| --text-xs--line-height | calc(1 / 0.75) |
| --text-sm | 0.875rem |
| --text-sm--line-height | calc(1.25 / 0.875) |
| --text-base | 1rem |
| --text-base--line-height | calc(1.5 / 1) |
